## Summary

The exact DSMZ 221 primary and catalogue confirm base identity. Twenty owner amount citations have derivative-only historical support. The generated target loses maintained evidence and preparation; two late 20% stocks are flattened, optional agar becomes an unconditional physical-state assertion, and generic source sodium malate does not establish the mapped racemate.

## Scientific And Domain Assessments

### Documented deterministic checks

schema: supported. Targets: CultureMech:004576.

The four documented recipes completed; interpret the scope of each separately.

### All asserted primary and current secondary chemical identifiers

grounding: concern. Targets: CultureMech:004576.

12 distinct primary/current-secondary IDs resolve to nonobsolete terms; exact stored/canonical labels are recorded separately in each term evidence item. Target direct-ingredient linkage is 12/13 primary (92.31%) and 12/13 current CHEBI-based secondary (92.31%), on a 0–100 linkage scale, not an accuracy score. Current secondary keys without pinned index rows: []. Legacy slot count 0 is separate and does not enter that current-key claim. Supplied-form correspondence is assessed separately.

### Append-only history and timestamp shape

provenance: concern. Targets: CultureMech:004576.

24 timestamp instances inspected; 4 malformed or timezone-naive instances. Historical import, repair and enrichment statements are provenance, not source confirmation. Preserve old events; append any later authorized clarification rather than silently rewriting historical records.

### Two maintained contributors and exact base accession

identity: supported. Targets: CultureMech:004576.

The exact catalogue 221 row links DSMZ 221 and authenticates the KOMODO owner identity attachment against matching raw bytes. Both explicit parent/child paths and stable IDs resolve. Current base identity is established; copied signatures and the local SOURCE_DUPLICATE relation do not independently authenticate historical formula bytes or a separate variant.

### Complex mixture, optional agar and catalogue oxygen metadata

scope: concern. Targets: CultureMech:004576.

Yeast extract supports complex/semi-defined classification and catalogue Is Complex=true. Agar is explicitly optional; the retained ingredient note does not justify presenting SOLID_AGAR as the only formulation without declaring that branch. Is Aerobic=false is verified catalogue metadata, not an organism physiological experiment. The genus title and application are not strain-specific growth evidence.

### Hydrates, malate specificity and mixture-link boundaries

grounding: concern. Targets: CultureMech:004576.

The specified FeSO4 heptahydrate, molybdate dihydrate, MnSO4 monohydrate, MgSO4 heptahydrate and CaCl2 dihydrate match their captured structures. Source Biotin agrees with the canonical biotin term, whose structure already includes stereochemistry; no generic-term defect is inferred. Glucose is unqualified in the source and local term. CHEBI:91260 explicitly describes an equimolar R/S disodium-malate racemate; source Na-malate does not establish that racemate or counterion stoichiometry. Yeast extract remains unlinked in both primary and secondary slots and needs an appropriate mixture-level mapping if grounded, without invented decomposition.

### Twenty retained owner claims and generated evidence loss

provenance: concern. Targets: CultureMech:004576.

All twenty existing amount snippets match decoded local plan claims; report lineage agrees. The original historical API response is unrecovered and the current API returned 503 twice. Those derivatives do not authenticate original provider g_l or volume. The generated target drops all maintained ingredient evidence and the DSMZ preparation. Current catalogue bytes separately authenticate the KOMODO identity attachment, not quantity claims.

## Findings

### f1: Late stocks and optional agar have unsupported unconditional mass representations

major / open / confirmed; issue key: cm004576-stock-scope.

The two 25 ml 20% late stocks are flattened to 5 g/L ingredients, and optional 1.5% agar to 15 g/L. Percent bases are unqualified; water input and stock structure are missing. The optional ingredient note survives, but SOLID_AGAR does not identify which branch was selected.

### f2: Generic sodium malate does not establish the mapped racemate

major / open / confirmed; issue key: cm004576-malate-specificity.

Both primary and secondary CHEBI:91260 refer to an equimolar R/S disodium-malate racemate. The source says Na-malate without stereochemical or counterion-stoichiometry qualification. Current MIM key membership is real but does not establish the supplied form.

### f3: Historical amount authentication and generated evidence retention remain incomplete

major / open / confirmed; issue key: cm004576-historical-evidence.

Twenty owner attachments reproduce historical curation claims in pinned derivatives, but their original response hash remains unrecovered. Current batch masses corroborate ingredient amounts without authenticating historical g_l metadata. The target loses all attachments and the full procedure already retained in the DSMZ owner.

## Recommended Actions And Acceptance Checks

### a1

Represent stock strengths, separate doses, filtration and late stage; preserve optional agar with explicit branch scope and unresolved percent basis. Do not silently derive final concentrations or choose a branch.

- Verify the exact source accession, ingredient form, original unit and batch/preparation context.
- Run just validate-schema, just validate-strict, just validate-terms and just validate-references on separately authorized maintained-input corrections.
- Regenerate derived records only after owner verification; run just verify-merges and just audit-merge-freshness and confirm corrected fields/evidence survive.

### a2

Verify the exact supplied sodium-malate form or qualify the grounding to the supported scope; do not infer a replacement identifier or racemate from generic wording.

- Verify the exact source accession, ingredient form, original unit and batch/preparation context.
- Run just validate-schema, just validate-strict, just validate-terms and just validate-references on separately authorized maintained-input corrections.
- Regenerate derived records only after owner verification; run just verify-merges and just audit-merge-freshness and confirm corrected fields/evidence survive.

### a3

Recover the exact original response or retain explicit historical authentication limits; attach current source evidence at the actual batch/stage scope and preserve owner preparation/evidence in later authorized regeneration.

- Verify the exact source accession, ingredient form, original unit and batch/preparation context.
- Run just validate-schema, just validate-strict, just validate-terms and just validate-references on separately authorized maintained-input corrections.
- Regenerate derived records only after owner verification; run just verify-merges and just audit-merge-freshness and confirm corrected fields/evidence survive.

- assessment_id: grounding
  area: grounding
  topic: All asserted primary and current secondary chemical identifiers
  outcome: concern
  summary: '12 distinct primary/current-secondary IDs resolve to nonobsolete terms;
    exact stored/canonical labels are recorded separately in each term evidence item.
    Target direct-ingredient linkage is 12/13 primary (92.31%) and 12/13 current CHEBI-based
    secondary (92.31%), on a 0–100 linkage scale, not an accuracy score. Current secondary
    keys without pinned index rows: []. Legacy slot count 0 is separate and does not
    enter that current-key claim. Supplied-form correspondence is assessed separately.'

- assessment_id: history
  area: provenance
  topic: Append-only history and timestamp shape
  outcome: concern
  summary: 24 timestamp instances inspected; 4 malformed or timezone-naive instances.
    Historical import, repair and enrichment statements are provenance, not source
    confirmation. Preserve old events; append any later authorized clarification rather
    than silently rewriting historical records.

- assessment_id: identity
  area: identity
  topic: Two maintained contributors and exact base accession
  outcome: supported
  summary: The exact catalogue 221 row links DSMZ 221 and authenticates the KOMODO
    owner identity attachment against matching raw bytes. Both explicit parent/child
    paths and stable IDs resolve. Current base identity is established; copied signatures
    and the local SOURCE_DUPLICATE relation do not independently authenticate historical
    formula bytes or a separate variant.
  target_ids:
  - CultureMech:004576
  evidence_ids:
  - record
  - owner-0
  - owner-1
  - primary
  - catalog
  - catalog-header
- assessment_id: scope
  area: scope
  topic: Complex mixture, optional agar and catalogue oxygen metadata
  outcome: concern
  summary: Yeast extract supports complex/semi-defined classification and catalogue
    Is Complex=true. Agar is explicitly optional; the retained ingredient note does
    not justify presenting SOLID_AGAR as the only formulation without declaring that
    branch. Is Aerobic=false is verified catalogue metadata, not an organism physiological
    experiment. The genus title and application are not strain-specific growth evidence.
  target_ids:
  - CultureMech:004576
  evidence_ids:
  - record
  - primary
  - catalog
  - catalog-header
  - chebi2509
- assessment_id: forms
  area: grounding
  topic: Hydrates, malate specificity and mixture-link boundaries
  outcome: concern
  summary: The specified FeSO4 heptahydrate, molybdate dihydrate, MnSO4 monohydrate,
    MgSO4 heptahydrate and CaCl2 dihydrate match their captured structures. Source
    Biotin agrees with the canonical biotin term, whose structure already includes
    stereochemistry; no generic-term defect is inferred. Glucose is unqualified in
    the source and local term. CHEBI:91260 explicitly describes an equimolar R/S disodium-malate
    racemate; source Na-malate does not establish that racemate or counterion stoichiometry.
    Yeast extract remains unlinked in both primary and secondary slots and needs an
    appropriate mixture-level mapping if grounded, without invented decomposition.
  target_ids:
  - CultureMech:004576
  evidence_ids:
  - primary
  - chebi75836
  - chebi75213
  - chebi86364
  - chebi31795
  - chebi86158
  - chebi15956
  - chebi17234
  - chebi91260
  - mim
  - record
- assessment_id: evidence
  area: provenance
  topic: Twenty retained owner claims and generated evidence loss
  outcome: concern
  summary: All twenty existing amount snippets match decoded local plan claims; report
    lineage agrees. The original historical API response is unrecovered and the current
    API returned 503 twice. Those derivatives do not authenticate original provider
    g_l or volume. The generated target drops all maintained ingredient evidence and
    the DSMZ preparation. Current catalogue bytes separately authenticate the KOMODO
    identity attachment, not quantity claims.
  target_ids:
  - CultureMech:004576
  evidence_ids:
  - record

  - owner-1

  - report-1

  - catalog
